Optimizer loop variants:

1. smooth_gradient_descent(p, n, time, beta_schedule, lr_schedule, rng):
   y=log(p+eps); y -= y.mean(); m=np.zeros; v=np.zeros; lr0 maybe 0.5? We need choose.
   best_local=score(p)
   for iterate:
     q=conv(p); qmax=q.max()
     # c maybe smoothed
     for beta in betas? Could alternate.
     phi, C=weights; if C<=0: C=qmax
     B=cross(phi,p)
     G=2*p*(B/C -1)
     # Optionally regularize to avoid zeros: + small entropy? maybe not.
     # Add small random noise to escape:
     if rng.random()<0.1: G += rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
     Adam:
       m=(1-beta1)m+beta1G; v=(1-beta2)v+beta2G²; m_hat=m/(1-beta1^t); v_hat=sqrt(v)/(1-beta2^t)
       y -= step * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
     y = center clip
     p=softmax(y)
     score =2n*qmax? recompute q? We already have q before; need after update compute qnew. Could use quick score qnew=conv(p).max()*2n.
     if score<best: best=p, y, lr maybe increase; else reduce? Adam not line search. We can do backtracking: try step multipliers [1,0.5,0.25,0.1] by updating y from y_old? But computing multiple q expensive. Instead use fixed lr and early stop.

Function `smooth_gradient_optimize(start_p, n, deadline, rng, verbose)` returns improved p,best_score.
Pseudo:

def smooth_gradient_optimize(p, n, deadline, rng, max_time_per=120):
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score(p)
    y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)); y -= y.mean()
    m = zeros(n); v=zeros(n); t=0
    lr = 0.1? 
    alphas = [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.5?]. We'll try.
    base_beta = n * 0.5? Need choose.
    while now<deadline:
       # maybe compute q from p for score and weights
       q = self_conv(p); qmax=q.max()
       if qmax <=0: break
       # choose beta: cyclic [n*0.2, n, n*5, n*20]
       beta = base_betas[t%len]
       exponents = beta*(q-qmax); exponents = np.where(exponents < -50, -inf, exponents)
       z=np.exp(exponents); z_sum=z.sum(); if z_sum<=0: phi = onehot idxmax
       else phi=z/z_sum
       C=qmax + np.log(z_sum)/beta if z_sum>0 else qmax
       # if C very small, C=qmax
       # compute B
       if sparse? if (phi>1e-8).sum() < max(2, n//100) and active_count*? maybe direct. But phi from soft may dense. For n<=2048, FFT okay. For active onehot, direct.
       if np.count_nonzero(phi > 0.1*phi.max()) <= 32:
           active = np.nonzero(phi > 1e-12)[0]; weights=phi[active]
           B = np.zeros(n)
           # direct loop over active
           for idx,w in zip(active,weights):
              k=idx
              lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
              B[lo:hi+1] += w * p[k-hi: k-lo+1][::-1]? Need mapping: for i in lo..hi, p[k-i]. We can extract p_slice = p[lo:hi+1]; need p[k-i] = p[k - (lo + r)] = p[k-lo-r]. This is reversed p[k-hi : k-lo+1]? Let's test: i=lo => p[k-lo], i=hi=>p[k-hi=k? wait hi=k if k<n, then p[k-hi]=p[0]. So array p[k-lo down to k-hi] reversed. We can do vals = p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]; B[lo:hi+1] += w*vals. Good.
       else:
           B = crosscorr(phi,p)
       G = 2.0*p*(B/C - 1.0)
       # Optional: subtract a small target max regularization? no.
       # Maybe add entropy/flatness? no.
       # Adam updates
       for alpha in alphas:
           y_trial = y - alpha*lr*m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
           # center/clip
           y_trial = y_trial - y_trial.mean(); y_trial=np.clip(y_trial, -CAP,CAP)
           p_trial = softmax(y_trial)
           q_trial = self_conv(p_trial); score = 2*n*q_trial.max()
           if score < best_score:
               best_score=score; best_p=p_trial; y=y_trial; p=p_trial
               # keep y? Need m,v from accepted? Use current G based on y_trial? Next iteration recompute.
               break
       else:
           # if no improvement, try random perturbation
           if rng.random()<0.2:
              y_trial = y + rng.normal(0, 0.1, n); center/clip; p_trial=softmax; score; if improved keep else reject
           # maybe reduce lr
       # update m,v with G even if rejected? Use accepted y's p? To save, update after decision:
       t+=1; m=0.9m+0.1G; v=0.999v+0.001G^2; ...
       if no improvement for many iterations: break or change params.
    return best_p,best_score

def surrogate_gradient_step(p,q,n):
   qmax=q.max(); A=1/q.size if q.size>0 else 1
   # high weights: choose beta such that exp range. Let's use beta = 20.0 (not scaled?) Need q dimension. Use beta = max(1.0, 40.0 / (qmax + 1e-12))? But qmax~1/n, 40/qmax=40n => exponent diff 40. Maybe too high. Use beta = 10 * n? Let's use beta = min(100000, 20.0 * n). For n=4000, 80000, exponent range up to 20 (if diff qmax). Good. z=exp(beta*(q-qmax)); z cap? Since <=1, Z <=len. C= qmax + log(Z)/beta.
   high = z
   # low weights: target A; low = A / max(q, 0.1A) maybe cap=10. If q=0, low=10.
   low = np.clip(A / np.maximum(q, A*0.05), 0.0, 20.0)
   phi = alpha_high*high + alpha_low*low. alpha_high maybe 1, alpha_low maybe 1. If qmax huge relative A? high sum maybe up to n; low sum up to n*? Could balance.
   C=φ @ q; if C<=0 use onehot max
   B=cross(φ,p)
   G=2p*(B/C -1)
   return G

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eval_best_seq=None; eval_best_score=np.inf
    global_best_p=None; global_best_score=np.inf; global_best_n=None
    # helper update_best(p,n,score): if score<global_best_score set; maybe eval_debug.

           update
    # Final local search on global best: small random multiplicative block perturbations with evaluate/exact
```

```
def make_weights(q, target, beta_high, beta_low, alpha_high=1.0, alpha_low=0.5):
    with np.errstate: logdev = np.log1p? 
    logdev = np.log(np.maximum(q, target*1e-6)) - np.log(target)
    high = np.exp(np.clip(beta_high * logdev, -50, 20))  # for high dev
    low = np.exp(np.clip(-beta_low * logdev, -50, 20))  # for low dev
    phi = alpha_high*high + alpha_low*low
    # Normalize? no.
```

```
for idx,w in zip(active, weights[active]):
```

Softmax function:
```
def softmax_y(y):
    y2 = y - y.max()
    e=np.exp(y2)
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return uniform
    return e/s
```
But if y mean centered and cap, max maybe 0. Use logsumexp stable.

When updating y with Adam, after centering/clip, p recompute. The step direction G tangent before centering. Centering y by mean doesn't affect p; good. If clip changes tangent, score line search authoritative.

Adam update code:
```
def adam_step(y, m, v, g, lr, t):
    m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g
    v = 0.999*v + 0.001*g*g
    mhat = m/(1-0.9**t)
    vhat = v/(1-0.999**t)
    dy = lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    y_new = y - dy
```
We can use beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999. For many iterations t up to 10000, bias small.

Line search alphas: We can try in order [1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0]. To save, compute dy base, then y_trial=y - alpha*dy.

If p_new has zeros due underflow? softmax exp of negative large but max stable, min exp(-cap) ~1e-13, positive. Good.

Let's detail `composite_gradient_optimize`:

```
def composite_gradient_optimize(p0, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=None):
    p = p0.copy(); p = normalize(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_from_p(p,n)
    current_score=best_score
    y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    no_improve=0; stuck_count=0
    param_sets = [...]
    proboscis...
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2 and stuck_count<20:
       # Pick params based on no_improve
       beta_h,beta_l,ah,al,lr = param_sets[... % len]
       # Optionally reset Adam if no_improve high
       if no_improve > 100:
           m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0
       q=self_conv(p,n); target=1/(2*n-1)
       logq=np.log(np.clip(q, target*1e-8, None)) - np.log(target)  # log dev
       # use actual q max in log? target.
       high=np.exp(np.clip(beta_h*logq, -30, 15))
       low=np.exp(np.clip(-beta_l*logq, -30, 15))
       phi = ah*high + al*low
       # maybe add small uniform base to keep C positive
       phi += 1e-6
       C = float(np.dot(phi,q))
       if not finite or C<=0: phi = np.zeros; phi[argmax]=1; C=qmax
       B = cross(weights, p)
       g = 2*p*(B/C - 1)
       # If g has nonfinite, break
       # Adam bias
       t+=1
       m = 0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.999*v+0.001*g*g
       mhat = m/(1-0.9**t); vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
       dy = lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       # If dy norm too large? scale?
       dy_scale = np.std(g)??
       improved=False
       for alpha in [1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25]:
           y_trial = y - alpha*dy
           y_trial = y_trial - y_trial.mean(); y_trial=np.clip(y_trial,-CAP,CAP)
           p_trial=softmax(y_trial)
           sc=score_from_p(p_trial,n)
           if sc < current_score:  # local improve
               y=y_trial; p=p_trial; current_score=sc
               if sc < best_score:
                   best_score=sc; best_p=p_trial.copy()
               no_improve=0; improved=True; break
          # maybe if sc < best only? no local.
       if not improved:
          no_improve +=1
          # Try random noises
          if no_improve in [20,50,100] or no_improve>100 and rng.random()<0.1:
              for sigma in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]:
                  y_rand=y+rng.normal(0,sigma,n); center clip; p_rand=...
                  sc=score;
                  if sc<current_score:
                      y=y_rand;p=p_rand;current_score=sc; if sc<best update; improved=True; break
              if improved: no_improve=0
              # If still no, and no_improve>maxstall (300), reset to best and large jump or break
              if not improved and no_improve>400:
                  # try large random from best
                  y_best = log(best_p); center; add noise 1.0; p=softmax; sc; if sc<best maybe
                  stuck_count +=1; break
       # optional periodic evaluate debug
    return best_p,best_score
```

Could using logdev relative target make weights independent of n? target ~1/n, logdev. For uniform, max dev=2 log=0.69, min near edges log very negative. β=5 gives low weights huge for edges (q~1/n² -> logdev= -log n ~ -8; low e40 huge capped 1e4) maybe too focused on tiny edges q, but edges count 2, okay. For pure sqrt, tail near end q~1/n² too, low huge may push endpoint. Good. But a few zero lags can dominate. Cap low at 1000 maybe. Use `np.clip(exp, 0, 10000)`.

High weights: For uniform center dev=2 -> e^3.5=33, cap okay. For pure first half dev~1.5 -> e^2.3=10.

Let's detail `make_weights` with q normalized:
```
def make_weights(q, profile):
    beta_h,beta_l,alpha_h,alpha_l=...
    L=len(q); target=1.0/L
    # avoid zeros: q_safe = np.maximum(q, target*1e-8)
    logdev = np.log(q_safe/target)
    # cap logdev to [-20,5] to keep exp limited
    logdev = np.clip(logdev, -20.0, 5.0)
    high = np.exp(beta_h * np.maximum(logdev, -20?))? If logdev negative, high small.
    low = np.exp(beta_l * (-logdev))
    high = np.minimum(high, 1e6); low=np.minimum(low,1e6)
    phi = alpha_h*high + alpha_l*low
    phi = np.maximum(phi, 1e-12)
    return phi
```

This penalizes both high and low while gradient direction? For high lag, B/C maybe high -> reduce; for low, B low -> increase. Good. It also gives nonzero weight to all. Maybe better than separate caps. Let φ = ah*exp(βh*trunc)+al*exp(βl*(-trunc)), but for high, low small; for low high small. At high dev, φ≈high; at low, φ≈low. Good.

`make_sqrt_prefix(n)`:
```
c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
```

`make_power_seq(n, beta, side, ...)`: Use vectorized:
```
def make_power_profile(n, left_exp, right_exp, shift=0.5):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    val=np.ones(n)
    if left_exp !=0: val /= np.power(i+shift, left_exp)
    if right_exp !=0: val /= np.power(n-i+shift, right_exp)
    return normalize(val)
```
If exp positive, high endpoints. left_exp 0.5 gives left high; right_exp 0.5 right high. Values maybe large near endpoint shift small; okay. Use shift=0.5.

Now `score_from_p`:
```
def score_from_p(p,n=None):
   p=np.asarray(p)
   if n is None: n=p.size
   if n<=0: return np.inf
   q=self_convolution(p)
   qmax=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
   if qmax<=0 or not finite: return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*qmax)
```
Since self_conv normalizes q to sum1.

```
def normalize_p(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0)
   p=np.maximum(p,0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(p.size)/p.size
   return p/s
```

`softmax_from_y`:
```
def softmax_from_y(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float)
   if y.size==0: return np.array([])
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
      y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=-1e3)
   ym=y.max()
   e=np.exp(y-ym)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(y.size)/y.size
   return e/s
```
Before exp, y centered/clip.

`random_block_perturb_y(y, rng, sigma, nblocks=None)`:
```
def perturb_y(y, rng, sigma):
   n=len(y)
   z=y.copy()
   # global small
   z += rng.normal(0, sigma*0.3, n)
   nb = int(rng.integers(1,4))
   for _ in range(nb):
       width = int(max(1, n * float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.2))))
       start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
       z[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0, sigma, width)
   # maybe reverse symmetry probability
   if rng.random()<0.2: z=0.5*(z+z[::-1])
   z -= z.mean()
   return np.clip(z, -LOG_CAP, LOG_CAP)
```
Returning y. But p softmax handles center; centering z to mean not necessary but good.

```
def composite_gradient_optimize(p0, n, local_deadline, rng):
    p=normalize_p(p0)
    best_p=p.copy()
    best_score=score_from_p(best_p,n)
    y=np.log(np.maximum(best_p,1e-300)); y-=y.mean()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    no_improve=0; iter=0; profile_idx=rng.integers(0,len(PROFILES))
    alphas=[0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]
    stall_limit=300
    print(f"  start n={n} score={best_score:.6f}")
    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
        iter+=1
        if no_improve > stall_limit:
            break
        if no_improve == 80 or no_improve == 160:
            profile_idx = (profile_idx+1) % len(PROFILES)
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0
        # every 200 and no improve, random restart from best with noise
        if no_improve > 50 and (no_improve % 25 ==0 or rng.random()<0.02):
            for sigma in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
                y_rand=perturb_y(y, rng, sigma)
                p_rand=softmax_from_y(y_rand)
                sc_rand=score_from_p(p_rand,n)
                if sc_rand < best_score:
                    best_p=p_rand; best_score=sc_rand; y=y_rand; no_improve=0; break
            if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline: break
        # compute gradient from best
        q=self_convolution(best_p)
        target=1.0/len(q)
        logdev=np.log(np.maximum(q, target*1e-8)) - np.log(target)
        logdev=np.clip(logdev, -12.0, 6.0)
        bh,bl,ah,al,lr=PROFILES[profile_idx]
        phi = ah*np.exp(np.clip(bh*logdev, -40, 30)) + al*np.exp(np.clip(-bl*logdev, -40, 30)) + 1e-8
        # cap phi maybe
        phi = np.minimum(phi, 1e6)
        C = float(np.dot(phi, q))
        if not finite C or C <=0:
           idx=int(np.argmax(q)); phi=np.zeros(len(q)); phi[idx]=1.0; C=float(q[idx])
        B=cross_corr_weights(phi,best_p)
        g=2.0*best_p*(B/C - 1.0)
        if not finite all: break
        t+=1
        m=0.9*m+0.1*g
        v=0.999*v+0.001*g*g
        b1=0.9; b2=0.999
        mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
        vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
        dy=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        # If dy huge, scale? 
        # Try alphas
        improved=False
        best_trial=None; best_trial_score=best_score
        for alpha in alphas:
            y_trial = y - alpha*dy
            y_trial = y_trial - y_trial.mean()
            y_trial = np.clip(y_trial, -LOG_CAP, LOG_CAP)
            p_trial=softmax_from_y(y_trial)
            sc_trial=score_from_p(p_trial,n)
            if sc_trial < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score=sc_trial; best_trial=(y_trial,p_trial,sc_trial)
        if best_trial is not None:
            y_trial,p_trial,sc_trial=best_trial
            best_p=p_trial; best_score=sc_trial; y=y_trial; no_improve=0
            if iter < 50 or iter % 50 ==0 or sc_trial < best_score -? print each improve? We already set best trial as best_score. Print if improve >1e-5:
        else:
            no_improve +=1
    return best_p, best_score
```

```python
"""
Heuristic search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The score is scale-invariant, so candidates are maintained normalized
to sum 1.  The code starts from several analytic/random profiles,
including the inverse-binomial prefix whose self-convolution is flat on
the first half.  Then it performs log-space multiplicative/subgradient
updates with data-dependent weights that penalize both very high and
very low convolution coefficients, plus random block/log perturbations.

Only `propose_candidate` is required by the driver.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


_LOG_CAP = 30.0
_EPS = 1e-300
PROFILES = [
    # (beta_high, beta_low, alpha_high, alpha_low, adam_lr)
    (3.0, 3.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.20),
    (5.0, 5.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.25),
    (8.0, 8.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.20),
    (5.0, 5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.25),
    (5.0, 5.0, 1.0, 2.0, 0.25),
    (10.0, 3.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.15),
    (3.0, 10.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.15),
    (6.0, 6.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.45),
    (12.0, 12.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.12),
]


def next_fft_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int((m - 1).bit_length())


def normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1:
        p = p.ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def softmax_from_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(y.size)
    return e / s


def self_convolution(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    length = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_fft_len(length)
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)
    q = q[:length]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        q = q / s
    return q


def score_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    q = self_convolution(p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * qmax)


def cross_corr_weights(weights, p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    expected_len = 2 * n - 1
    if weights.size != expected_len:
        tmp = np.zeros(expected_len, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(weights.size, expected_len)
        tmp[:m] = weights[:m]
        weights = tmp

    wmax = float(np.max(weights)) if weights.size else 0.0
    threshold = max(wmax * 1e-8, 1e-300)
    active = np.nonzero(weights > threshold)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(weights))], dtype=int)

    # Sparse direct path: O(n * k)
    if active.size <= 64:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for idx in active:
            k = int(idx)
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if hi < lo:
                continue
            start = k - lo
            end = k - hi
            vals = p[end:start + 1][::-1]
            out[lo:hi + 1] += weights[k] * vals
        return out

    # Dense FFT path.
    full_len = 3 * n - 2
    M = next_fft_len(full_len)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(weights[::-1], M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, M)
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def make_weights_from_q(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    L = q.size
    target = 1.0 / float(L)
    qsafe = np.maximum(q, target * 1e-10)
    logdev = np.log(qsafe / target)
    logdev = np.clip(logdev, -12.0, 6.0)
    # Shape depends on profile chosen outside; this helper returns logdev.
    return logdev


def compose_phi(logdev, beta_high, beta_low, alpha_high, alpha_low):
    phi = (
        alpha_high * np.exp(np.clip(beta_high * logdev, -40.0, 30.0))
        + alpha_low * np.exp(np.clip(-beta_low * logdev, -40.0, 30.0))
        + 1e-8
    )
    return np.minimum(phi, 1e5)


def make_sqrt_prefix(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
    return normalize_p(c)


def make_power_profile(n, left_exp, right_exp):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    shifted = i + 0.5
    rshifted = float(n) - i + 0.5
    val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if left_exp != 0.0:
        val = val / np.power(shifted, left_exp)
    if right_exp != 0.0:
        val = val / np.power(rshifted, right_exp)
    return normalize_p(val)


def make_random_smooth_p(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
    y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.8) * t
    y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.8) * (t * t)
    y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.5) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.4) * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)
    return softmax_from_y(np.clip(y - y.mean(), -10.0, 10.0))


def make_random_block_p(n, rng):
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for _ in range(rng.integers(2, 5)):
        width = int(max(1, n * float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.35))))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        p[start:stop] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45)))
    return normalize_p(p)


def make_candidate_starts(n, rng, count=10):
    starts = []
    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    starts.append(make_sqrt_prefix(n))

    base = make_sqrt_prefix(n)
    for delta in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
        starts.append(normalize_p(base + delta * base[::-1]))

    for beta in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
        starts.append(make_power_profile(n, beta, 0.0))
    for beta in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
        starts.append(make_power_profile(n, 0.0, beta))
    for a, b in ((0.20, 0.20), (0.30, 0.30), (0.10, 0.30), (0.30, 0.10)):
        starts.append(make_power_profile(n, a, b))

    for _ in range(4):
        starts.append(make_random_smooth_p(n, rng))
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(make_random_block_p(n, rng))

    scored = []
    for p in starts:
        p = normalize_p(p)
        s = score_from_p(p)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, p))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    return [p for _, p in scored[:max(1, count)]]


def parent_sequence_global():
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n < 32 or n > 25000:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def perturb_y(y, rng, sigma):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = y.size
    if n <= 0:
        return y
    y += rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-6, 0.3 * sigma), n)
    nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        width = int(max(1, n * float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.25))))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        y[start:stop] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, stop - start)
    if rng.random() < 0.15:
        y = 0.5 * (y + y[::-1])
    return np.clip(y - float(np.mean(y)), -LOG_CAP, LOG_CAP)


def center_y(y):
    return np.clip(np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64) - float(np.mean(np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64))),
                    -LOG_CAP, LOG_CAP)


def composite_gradient_optimize(p0, n, local_deadline, rng):
    p_best = normalize_p(p0)
    score_best = score_from_p(p_best)
    if not np.isfinite(score_best):
        p_best = make_sqrt_prefix(n)
        score_best = score_from_p(p_best)

    y = np.log(np.maximum(p_best, 1e-300))
    y = center_y(y)
    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    variance = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    adam_t = 0
    profile_idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(PROFILES)))
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = score_best
    alphas = (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)

    print(
        f"[composite] start n={n} score={score_best:.6f}",
        flush=True,
    )

    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
        if no_improve >= 320:
            break

        # Periodically change profile / reset Adam if stalled.
        if no_improve == 80 or no_improve == 160 or no_improve == 240:
            profile_idx = int((profile_idx + 1) % len(PROFILES))
            momentum.fill(0.0)
            variance.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            y = np.log(np.maximum(p_best, 1e-300))
            y = center_y(y)

        # Random exploratory jumps around the best point.
        if no_improve > 40 and (no_improve % 20 == 0 or rng.random() < 0.02):
            tried = False
            for sigma in (0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.70):
                y_rand = perturb_y(y, rng, sigma)
                p_rand = softmax_from_y(y_rand)
                s_rand = score_from_p(p_rand)
                if s_rand < score_best:
                    p_best = p_rand
                    score_best = s_rand
                    y = y_rand
                    no_improve = 0
                    tried = True
                    if score_best < last_print - 1e-5:
                        print(f"[composite] random improve n={n} score={score_best:.6f}", flush=True)
                        last_print = score_best
                    break
            if tried:
                continue

        q = self_convolution(p_best)
        logdev = make_weights_from_q(q)
        beta_high, beta_low, alpha_high, alpha_low, lr = PROFILES[profile_idx]
        phi = compose_phi(logdev, beta_high, beta_low, alpha_high, alpha_low)
        C = float(np.dot(phi, q))
        if (not np.isfinite(C)) or C <= 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(q))
            phi = np.zeros_like(q)
            phi[idx] = 1.0
            C = float(q[idx])

        B = cross_corr_weights(phi, p_best)
        g = 2.0 * p_best * (B / max(C, 1e-300) - 1.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        adam_t += 1
        b1 = 0.9
        b2 = 0.999
        momentum = (1.0 - b1) * g + b1 * momentum
        variance = (1.0 - b2) * (g * g) + b2 * variance
        m_hat = momentum / (1.0 - b1 ** adam_t)
        v_hat = variance / (1.0 - b2 ** adam_t)
        dy = lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(dy)):
            break

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_score = score_best
        for alpha in alphas:
            y_trial = y - alpha * dy
            y_trial = center_y(y_trial)
            p_trial = softmax_from_y(y_trial)
            s_trial = score_from_p(p_trial)
            if np.isfinite(s_trial) and s_trial < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score = s_trial
                best_trial = (y_trial, p_trial, s_trial)

        if best_trial is not None:
            y_trial, p_trial, s_trial = best_trial
            p_best = p_trial
            score_best = s_trial
            y = y_trial
            no_improve = 0
            if score_best < last_print - 1e-5:
                print(f"[composite] improve n={n} score={score_best:.6f}", flush=True)
                last_print = score_best
        else:
            no_improve += 1

    return p_best, score_best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    global_best = None
    global_best_score = float("inf")
    global_best_n = 0

    print(f"[top] budget={budget:.0f}s", flush=True)

    # Build an initial gallery of starting points.
    gallery = []

    # Lengths: enough surface area, but still fast for 2,400 seconds.
    lengths = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384]
    # If the provided sequence is moderate, include it too.
    par = parent_sequence_global()
    if par is not None and par.size not in lengths:
        lengths.append(int(par.size))

    quick_limit = deadline - 180.0
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > quick_limit:
            break
        if n < 32 or n > 16384:
            continue
        count = 8 if n > 8192 else 12
        starts = make_candidate_starts(n, rng, count=count)
        for p in starts:
            s = score_from_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                gallery.append((s, p, int(n)))

    # Add the global sequence when valid.
    if par is not None:
        s = score_from_p(par)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            gallery.append((s, normalize_p(par), int(par.size)))

    if not gallery:
        n0 = 2048
        p0 = make_sqrt_prefix(n0)
        global_best = p0
        global_best_score = score_from_p(p0)
        global_best_n = n0
        gallery = [(global_best_score, global_best, global_best_n)]

    gallery.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    def update_global(p, s, n):
        nonlocal global_best, global_best_score, global_best_n
        if p is None or s is None or not np.isfinite(s):
            return
        s = float(s)
        if s < global_best_score - 1e-12:
            global_best = normalize_p(p).copy()
            global_best_score = s
            global_best_n = int(n)
            print(f"[top] global best updated: n={n} score={s:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Optimize promising starting points.  We use a small queue and add
    # perturbed copies of improvements so the best basin can be polished.
    queue = [(s, p, int(n)) for (s, p, n) in gallery]
    seen_polish = 0
    processed = 0

    while queue and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        s, p, n = queue.pop(0)
        if n <= 0 or n > 16384:
            continue
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break

        # If we already have a good solution, ignore very bad starts.
        if global_best_score < 1.62 and s > global_best_score + 0.12 and processed >= 2:
            continue

        # Time chunk: roughly one of the next 3-4 blocks.
        if remaining > 900.0:
            chunk = 280.0
        elif remaining > 450.0:
            chunk = 220.0
        elif remaining > 220.0:
            chunk = 150.0
        else:
            chunk = max(45.0, remaining - 20.0)

        local_deadline = min(deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + chunk)
        try:
            p_opt, s_opt = composite_gradient_optimize(p, n, local_deadline, rng)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[top] optimizer failed: {exc}", flush=True)
            continue

        update_global(p_opt, s_opt, n)
        processed += 1

        # If this produced an improvement, queue a mild perturbation of it
        # for polishing.  Limit this to avoid unbounded growth.
        if s_opt < global_best_score + 1e-12 and global_best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
            if seen_polish < 12:
                yb = np.log(np.maximum(global_best, 1e-300))
                for sigma in (0.04, 0.10):
                    y_new = perturb_y(yb, rng, sigma)
                    p_new = softmax_from_y(y_new)
                    s_new = score_from_p(p_new)
                    if np.isfinite(s_new):
                        queue.append((s_new, p_new, int(global_best.size)))
                        seen_polish += 1
                        if seen_polish >= 12:
                            break

        if global_best_score <= 1.5035 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            # We are already essentially at target.  Keep polishing the best
            # basin for the rest of the time.
            if global_best is not None:
                small_chunk = min(deadline - 60.0, max(45.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0))
                try:
                    p_pol, s_pol = composite_gradient_optimize(
                        global_best,
                        int(global_best.size),
                        small_chunk,
                        rng,
                    )
                    update_global(p_pol, s_pol, p_pol.size)
                except Exception as exc:
                    print(f"[top] polish failed: {exc}", flush=True)

    # Final safe fallback: ensure we return a valid finite nonnegative list.
    if global_best is None or global_best.size <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(global_best)):
        global_best = make_sqrt_prefix(2048)
        global_best_score = score_from_p(global_best)
        global_best_n = global_best.size

    global_best = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64)
    global_best = np.where(np.isfinite(global_best), global_best, 0.0)
    global_best = np.maximum(global_best, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(global_best))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        global_best = make_sqrt_prefix(2048)
        s = float(np.sum(global_best))
    if s > 0.0:
        global_best = global_best / s

    # Final validation/print; this does not change the returned candidate.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
            if ev is not None and global_best.size <= 16384:
                final_score = ev([float(x) for x in global_best])
                print(f"[top] final internal score={global_best_score:.6f}, evaluator={final_score:.6f}", flush=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[top] final validation skipped: {exc}", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in global_best]
```
